  - 包/命令：`aesfix` · 安装：`sudo apt install aesfix` · 官网：<https://citp.princeton.edu/our-work/memory/code/>

  - 包/命令：`xmount` · 安装：`sudo apt install xmount` · 官网：<https://www.sits.lu/xmount>
